## Status and evidence rules

- `closed` can retain an explicit bounded residual when the accepted test receipt names the item and its profile. It does not claim universal closure.
- `partial` records either an explicit incomplete property or a specific absence of item-bound accepted evidence; each row says which. `UNRUN` is not a pass and does not mean environmental red.
- Source-card, package-level acceptance and triage records alone do not close a finding. The 71 E02 package receipts are retained as bundle-level context but their finding-level behavioral verdict is UNRUN; each row cites the source-card discriminator and record-inspection probe. LA-021 cites the separate reviewed bounded probe.
- B73 remains partial: the expected-red clean-782 probe reproduced the legacy-hook split (`right=2` persisted while `completed_nodes=["left"]`). Commit `bac6996` fences the unsafe node-only fallback, with bounded 11/11 focused and 9/9 whole-file evidence. Four-base replay, wider fail-fast/continue durable-head behavior, and arbitrary custom-hook persistence semantics remain UNRUN.

| LA-025 | SCL-03 | partial | partial | — | a: Resolve a retained v1 snapshot reference from the existing CAS and prove the cache hit reads those exact bytes without network access. A same-URL v2 snapshot is a distinct reference and must not replace v1; if the blob is gone or access is denied, return a typed gap or an explicit refresh with new lineage. Preserve source identity and prove the behavior through the existing consumer. | Production empty-seed search→enrich already carries content-addressed bytes: the API persists the WebEvidenceBundle, resolves the selected source to `SourceSpec(kind=bytes)`, reads CAS content by ArtifactID and verifies digest/size; `_acquire_bytes` verifies again without URL re-fetch. The remaining defect is source-metadata binding. `build_source_metadata` copies content digest/type but omits the same FetchResult’s ArtifactRef/artifact_id, byte_size, final_url, redirect_chain, ETag, Last-Modified, license, fetch_profile, lineage_parent_artifact_id and refresh_reason. SourceMetadata derives only an ArtifactID from the digest; the other fields stay at defaults. `_source_spec_from_snapshot` fills missing artifact_id, final_url, redirect_chain, ETag, Last-Modified, fetch_profile, refresh lineage, byte_size and license from `cache.get(source_url)`, which is the URL’s current record rather than the retained source snapshot. A retained v1 can therefore keep v1 bytes while inheriting same-URL v2 metadata; if v2 byte_size differs, the API rejects the valid v1 bytes. The handoff also reduces full ArtifactRef to ArtifactID, losing kind/media_type/manifest_profile_sha256. The live fetcher leaves effective fetch_profile at its default `{}`; DocSourceSpec/DocMeta do not persist fetch profile or refresh lineage, and WebEvidenceBundle retains only fields its producer supplied.
